    - generic: ". Directly locating a Hidden creature defaults to Perception against its recorded final Stealth total as a static DC, using task degrees/natural shifts. Success reveals location only to the searcher and ends Hidden for that observer, without automatically granting sight. Communicate normally to inform others. Search can be repeated on later turns by paying the Action. Attacks against unseen targets have disadvantage; unseen attackers have advantage, subject to the **Core Invisible exception**: once its position is known, Invisible's own attack modifiers end for that observer and cannot immediately return solely because it remains Invisible. Independent darkness, smoke, blindness or hiding can still supply modifiers. Resolve an attack using pre-attack information; afterward reveal location to observers able to perceive its evidence. If an unseen target's location is unknown, do not directly choose it for a creature-target effect. A weapon/projectile can instead attack a suspected space: wrong space automatically misses; correct space uses unseen rules. A “creatu FILE DescentReviewChanges/ReviewBible/docs/mechanics/SPELL_INTEGRATION_RULES.md ctive. Same-stat flat bonuses use the strongest; same-name dice bonuses do not stack. Apply one allowed bonus-die spell per roll in this packet unless a reviewed feature explicitly permits more. Temporary HP never add together. Copies never generate copies, class procs or resource loops. ## Falling bridge — new provisional procedure This is a newly authored table procedure agreed with the GPT notebook, **not recovered V6/Core V2 canon**. Focused searches of the current rulebook extract, Core V2 and mechanics/manual text found spell summaries but no operational falling clock or impact formula. Before promotion to canon, inspect the approved V6 environment/travel/hazard sections and archived environment tables. Preserve any older rule found and compare it explicitly. When a creature loses support with no movement mode or effect keeping it aloft, it enters **Falling** immediately. Falling means unsupported; it grants no hovering, airborne walking or lateral movement. Track total distance descended during this fall and cumulative distance during its current uncontrolled portion, both initially zero. **Timing:** If a fall begins during a regular initiative turn, set a fixed Fall Marker at the **end of that initiative slot in the following round**. Resolve the first Falling Step there, then one at the same marker each round. Reactions and extra turns use the enclosing regular initiative slot; an event between slots uses the most recently completed slot. Extra turns, extra Actions and initiative changes neither move nor multiply the marker. This gives every participant one normal turn opportunity before the first descent; inability to act still matters and no free rescue action is granted. A caster still needs legal targets within range when spending their Action. Outside initiative, when timing matters, establish an order before descent and use a virtual slot immediately before its first regular slot as the initiating slot. The first step occurs when that virtual slot next recurs, after everyone has had one normal turn opportunity. When no rescue or other timing decision is possible, the Director can resolve the same descent and impact calculation directly. This is an explicit timing abstraction, not a model of physical acceleration. If a fall begins after initiative has been established but before any regular initiative slot has yet completed, treat the virtual pre-round slot as the most recently completed slot for Fall Marker purposes. **Ordinary descent:** At each step descend up to **300 feet**, stopping on a supporting surface. Add the distance to both trackers. On landing while uncontrolled, take **min(10, floor(uncontrolled distance / 20))d6 bludgeoning damage**. The uncontrolled distance includes earlier steps; never use only the final step's remainder. **Feather Fall:** The spell retains its **Action** casting time and reduces descent to at most **60 feet per step**. When it takes effect, set uncontrolled distance to zero: the magic dissipates prior uncontrolled momentum in this abstraction. Keep total fall distance. If landing while it remains active, damage is **min(3, floor(total fall distance / 20))d6 bludgeoning**. This explicitly limits impact exposure without guaranteeing a harmless landing. When the spell ends aloft, reset uncontrolled distance to zero, then accumulate ordinary descent from that point at the existing marker; expiry grants no immediate extra step. Its one-minute maximum ends immediately before the caster's regular turn ten rounds after casting. If expiry and a step coincide, resolve expiry first. Earlier landing or cessation of Falling ends the spell on that target as printed. Apply damage defenses only when their actual wording covers that damage. After damage, Falling ends; a creature suffering any positive falling damage lands **Prone** unless an explicit feature prevents it. Less than 20 relevant feet produces zero damage. Forced movement that removes support starts a fall but adds no separate fall damage. Regaining genuine support, secure support from a hold, or flight ends Falling without resolving an uncompleted impact; teleporting safely onto support likewise ends it. A later loss of support begins a new fall with new trackers and marker. Completed impact and damage cannot be undone by later casting. | Scenario | Steps to land | Impact | |---|---:|---:| | Ordinary 10 feet | 1 | 0 | | Ordinary 20 feet | 1 | 1d6 | | Ordinary 300 feet | 1 | 10d6 | | Ordinary 310 feet | 2 | 10d6 | | Ordinary 610 feet | 3 | 10d6 | | Feather Fall active through a 310-foot fall | 6 | 3d6 | | 120 feet slowed, spell ends aloft, then 190 feet uncontrolled | 3 | 9d6 | *. Resolve the first Falling Step there, then one at the same marker each round. Reactions and extra turns use the enclosing regular initiative slot; an event between slots uses the most recently completed slot. Extra turns, extra Actions and initiative changes neither move nor multiply the marker. This gives every participant one normal turn opportunity before the first descent; inability to act still matters and no free rescue action is granted. A caster still needs legal targets within range when spending their Action. Outside initiative, when timing matters, establish an order before descent and use a virtual slot immediately before its first regular slot as the initiating slot. The first step occurs when that virtual slot next recurs, after everyone has had one normal turn opportunity. When no rescue or other timing decision is possible, the Director can resolve the same descent and impact calculation directly. This is an explicit timing abstraction, not a model of physical acceleration. If a fall begins after initiative has been established but before any regular initiative slot has yet completed, treat the virtual pre-round slot as the most recently completed slot for Fall Marker purposes. **Ordinary descent:** At each step descend up to **300 feet**, stopping on a supporting surface. Add the distance to both trackers. On landing while uncontrolled, take **min(10, floor(uncontrolled distance / 20))d6 bludgeoning damage**. The uncontrolled distance includes earlier steps; never use only the final step's remainder. **Feather Fall:** The spell retains its **Action** casting time and reduces descent to at most **60 feet per step**. When it takes effect, set uncontrolled distance to zero: the magic dissipates prior uncontrolled momentum in this abstraction. Keep total fall distance. If landing while it remains active, damage is **min(3, floor(total fall distance / 20))d6 bludgeoning**. This explicitly limits impact exposure without guaranteeing a harmless landing. When the spell ends aloft, reset uncontrolled distance to zero, then accumulate ordinary descent from that point at the existing marker; expiry grants no immediate extra step. Its one-minute maximum ends immediately before the caster's regular turn ten rounds after casting. If expiry and a step coincide, resolve expiry first. Earlier landing or cessation of Falling ends the spell on that target as printed. Apply damage defenses only when their actual wording covers that damage. After damage, Falling ends; a creature suffering any positive falling damage lands **Prone** unless an explicit feature prevents it. Less than 20 relevant feet produces zero damage. Forced movement that removes support starts a fall but adds no separate fall damage. Regaining genuine support, secure support from a hold, or flight ends Falling without resolving an uncompleted impact; teleporting safely onto support likewise ends it. A later loss of support begins a new fall with new trackers and marker. Retain the expedition rule of at most **two beneficial short rests between long rests**, and one beneficial eight-hour long rest per **24 hours**. A long rest restores RP and clears Dissonance; expedition time, supply, pressure, Wounds and rest legality still apply. Count time once, not once in each tracker. Oracle and other non-RP engines use their class-specific recovery, not this formula. This bridge explicitly chooses the class Retune formula over the gameplay draft's quarter-pool-only RP paragraph. Both originals remain preserved. A completed long rest also resets the remembered spell name and Refrain counter. Same-stat flat bonuses use the strongest; same-name dice bonuses do not stack. Apply one allowed bonus-die spell per roll in this packet unless a reviewed feature explicitly permits more. Temporary HP never add together. Copies never generate copies, class procs or resource loops. ## Falling bridge — new provisional procedure This is a newly authored table procedure agreed with the GPT notebook, **not recovered V6/Core V2 canon**. Focused searches of the current rulebook extract, Core V2 and mechanics/manual text found spell summaries but no operational falling clock or impact formula. Before promotion to canon, inspect the approved V6 environment/travel/hazard sections and archived environment tables. Preserve any older rule found and compare it explicitly. When a creature loses support with no movement mode or effect keeping it aloft, it enters **Falling** immediately. Falling means unsupported; it grants no hovering, airborne walking or lateral movement. Track total distance descended during this fall and cumulative distance during its current uncontrolled portion, both initially zero. **Timing:** If a fall begins during a regular initiative turn, set a fixed Fall Marker at the **end of that initiative slot in the following round**. Resolve the first Falling Step there, then one at the same marker each round. Reactions and extra turns use the enclosing regular initiative slot; an event between slots uses the most recently completed slot. Extra turns, extra Actions and initiative changes neither move nor multiply the marker. This gives every participant one normal turn opportunity before the first descent; inability to act still matters and no free rescue action is granted. A caster still needs legal targets within range when spending their Action. Outside initiative, when timing matters, establish an order before descent and use a virtual slot immediately before its first regular slot as the initiating slot. The first step occurs when that virtual slot next recurs, after everyone has had one normal turn opportunity. When no rescue or other timing decision is possible, the Director can resolve the same descent and impact calculation directly. This is an explicit timing abstraction, not a model of physical acceleration. If a fall begins after initiative has been established but before any regular initiative slot has yet completed, treat the virtual pre-round slot as the most recently completed slot for Fall Marker purposes. **Ordinary descent:** At each step descend up to **300 feet**, stopping on a supporting surface. Add the distance to both trackers. On landing while uncontrolled, take **min(10, floor(uncontrolled distance / 20))d6 bludgeoning damage**. The uncontrolled distance includes earlier steps; never use only the final step's remainder. **Feather Fall:** The spell retains its **Action** casting time and reduces descent to at most **60 feet per step**. When it takes effect, set uncontrolled distance to zero: the magic dissipates prior uncontrolled momentum in this abstraction. Keep total fall distance. If landing while it remains active, damage is **min(3, floor(total fall distance / 20))d6 bludgeoning**. This explicitly limits impact exposure without guaranteeing a harmless landing. When the spell ends aloft, reset uncontrolled distance to zero, then accumulate ordinary descent from that point at the existing marker; expiry grants no immediate extra step. Its one-minute maximum ends immediately before the caster's regular turn ten rounds after casting. If expiry and a step coincide, resolve expiry first. Earlier landing or cessation of Falling ends the spell on that target as printed. Apply damage defenses only when their actual wording covers that damage. After damage, Falling ends; a creature suffering any positive falling damage lands **Prone** unless an explicit feature prevents it. Less than 20 relevant feet produces zero damage.       - text: "):"
      - generic: "\"\"\"Render nested current procedure/readable dict.\"\"\""
      - text: out=[]
      - generic: "if isinstance(v,str):"

      - generic: out.append("")
      - generic: "elif isinstance(v,(int,float,bool)) or v is None:"
      - generic: out.append(str(v))
      - generic: out.append("")
      - generic: "elif isinstance(v,list):"
      - generic: "for item in v:"
      - generic: "if isinstance(item,(str,int,float,bool)) or item is None:"
      - text: out.append(
      - generic: "f\"- {item}\""
      - text: )
      - generic: "else:"
      - generic: "# nested object as compact bullets"
      - generic: rendered=md_value(item,level+1)
      - generic: out.append("- " + rendered[0] if rendered else "-")

      - generic: out.append("")
      - generic: "elif isinstance(v,dict):"
      - generic: "for k,val in v.items():"
      - generic: title=re.sub(r'([a-z0-9])([A-Z])',r'\1 \2',k).replace("_"," ").strip().title()
      - text: out.append("#"*min(level,6)+
      - generic: "f\" {title}\""
      - text: )
      - generic: out.append("")
      - generic: out.extend(md_value(val,level+1))
      - generic: return out

      - generic: "# compute AABB euclidean distance from geometry ledger"
      - generic: geom=c["geometryLedger"]["bodyVolumes"]
      - generic: "def aabb_dist(a,b):"
      - generic: amin,amax=geom[a]["min"],geom[a]["max"]
      - generic: bmin,bmax=geom[b]["min"],geom[b]["max"]
      - text: ds=[]
      - generic: "for i in range(3):"
      - generic: "if amax[i] < bmin[i]:"
      - text: ds.append(bmin[i]-amax[i])
      - generic: "elif bmax[i] < amin[i]:"
      - text: ds.append(amin[i]-bmax[i])
      - generic: "else:"
      - generic: ds.append(0)
      - generic: return math.sqrt(sum(d*d for d in ds))
      - generic: ids=list(geom)
      - generic: "for prev in [\"A\",\"F\",\"U\",\"D\",\"E\"]:"
      - generic: "vals={x:aabb_dist(prev,x) for x in ids if x!=prev}"
      - generic: "print(prev, {k:round(v,9) for k,v in vals.items() if v<=30})"

These dependencies remain open. | ## Encounter and action resources Establish terrain, light, hazards, objectives, exits and cargo; determine awareness; roll `d20 + DEX modifier + legal bonuses`. Highest first. Players choose player/player ties; the Director resolves ties involving NPCs. Keep the resulting ordered slots for the encounter. A round represents approximately six seconds. A **regular turn** is a creature's normal initiative slot even if it cannot act. An extra turn is distinct. A turn normally permits movement, one Action, a Bonus Action when an actual rule grants a use, and one ordinary free object interaction. A second ordinary interaction or an Action-time object activation uses an Action. Brief communication is not an extra Action. Conditions and specific features may restrict these permissions. The shared limit is one **extra full Action per creature per numbered round**. Such an Action cannot grant another extra Action, Bonus Action or Reaction. This cap is independent of Reaction timing. Extra turns do not by themselves refresh Reactions, recharge, periodic ticks, recovery ticks or the regular-turn allowances defined here. **Reaction interpretation.** Begin an encounter with one available Reaction unless surprised or otherwise prohibited. Refresh at each regular own-turn start; never at the numbered round boundary or an extra turn. A late-initiative creature can therefore react before its first turn, refresh, and react again in the same numbered round. A legal trigger, available resource and all feature prerequisites are still required. **Explicit additional Reactions.** A reviewed card can grant a separate limited use without refreshing the ordinary resource. Warrior Mind Shock supplies its stated once-per-duration non-attack Reaction; spending one resource does not consume/reset the other. Surprise, Incapacitated and Focused still restrict both. It grants no Counterspell knowledge or waived costs. Extra turns refresh neither. Per-dose reapplication/stacking and other specific grants remain part of the class semantic pass. **Surprise — V6 plus NEW Bonus Action clarification.** On a surprised creature's first regular turn it cannot voluntarily move or take an Action or Bonus Action. It cannot react until that turn ends. Its slot, durations, passive effects, concentration, periodic effects and recharge still occur. Surprise alone is not Incapacitated or Unconscious. Brief communication and an otherwise legal simple free interaction remain possible. **Entry — NEW.** Standard end-round reinforcements roll/receive initiative and first act next round. An explicit midround entrant acts this round only if its complete ordered slot is still upcoming; entry during that exact slot counts as already passed. It has its initial Reaction unless prohibited. A summon-specific timing rule overrides this generic entry procedure. After the final slot, resolve Core's end-round hazards, reinforcements, morale, objectives and boss phases. Conclude the scene with surrender, capture, pursuit, harvesting access and noise consequences under their own rules; winning initiative does not resolve those automatically. ## Time and event ordering — NEW For a duration created in initiative, record the relevant original regular start/end marker. Existing effects keep that **virtual marker**, repeating every six seconds if the creature leaves or changes initiative. Reordering, rerolling, extra turns, death/removal or leaving/reentering initiative never restart an existing duration. New effects use the current schedule. Source death still ends concentration, a source-dependent aura/tether or an effect explicitly ending on death. - A bare hostile target-bound **N rounds** ends at the Nth future regular target-end marker. An in-progress target turn's current end does not count. - A bare self/source buff, ally-targeted buff or source-controlled aura **N rounds** ends at the Nth future source-start marker. Explicit target-turn, pulse or absolute timing overrides this default. Activation during source turn 1 for three rounds ends at source turn 4's start. - **Next turn** means the next future regular turn of the named creature, not the current turn's still-upcoming end. An unqualified next turn refers to --- Hover --- on Speed changes, preserving spent cost. Stand from Prone costs floor(half current Speed) and requires nonzero Speed and enough budget. Dropping Prone costs no movement. For each foot traveled, normal/difficult/severe terrain costs 1/2/3; use strongest terrain only. Add +1 for crawling and +1 for squeezing. Crawl in difficult =3:1; crawl+squeeze in difficult =4:1. A nonhostile occupied space counts as difficult terrain. Enter a hostile creature's space only with ≥two-size difference or an express feature; never willingly end in an occupied space. Squeeze through a space one size smaller, with own attack/DEX-save disadvantage and attacks-against advantage. Walk10 through difficult spends20; switch to Fly30 leaves10. Stand with Speed30 spends15; switching to Fly30 leaves15. Two legal Dashes at Speed30 give cap90; prior expenditure still counts. Interpose/Press grant5 budget: they cannot enter a difficult 5-ft tile costing10. Their separate budgets never refill ordinary movement. Ready movement uses Speed at release; Withdraw uses half current Speed. All voluntary movement requires nonzero relevant Speed. Forced movement does not use these ledgers or provoke ordinary opportunity attacks, cannot place creatures in solid matter, and stops at obstacles. Teleport does not traverse intervening terrain or spend Speed unless printed; it requires a legal destination. Speed0 alone does not block forced movement or teleport. Flight with Prone/Speed0/lost support causes Falling unless Hover or an explicit magical support exception applies; use the existing fixed Fall Marker procedure. Reaching support ends Falling. NEW grid metric: each orthogonal or diagonal neighboring tile is 5 ft; use the same metric for ordinary ranges/areas. No diagonal cut through a corner when both adjacent orthogonal sides are completely blocked. Exact templates, 3D geometry, mounts, chases, jumping, climbing/swimming procedures and currents need further source review. ## Cover, sight, Hide and Search Keep **visibility**, **known location**, and **line of effect** separate. Seeing through a barrier does not itself permit attacking through it. Best cover only: half +2 AC/DEX saves; three-quarters +5; total prevents direct targeting through that obstruction. Never add +2 and +5. For areas, evaluate line/cover from the area's origin to each target; total cover there excludes a creature unless the effect explicitly travels around/through the obstruction. The origin point itself must be legally targetable. Within normal ranged range, no range penalty; beyond normal through long, disadvantage; beyond long, illegal. Ranged attacks also have disadvantage when within5ft of a non-Incapacitated hostile that can see the attacker, even if the attacker cannot see that hostile. All advantage/disadvantage follows the single cancellation rule. NEW **Hide**: permitted only against observers unable to see you clearly or where a feature permits. Roll DEX Stealth once, record natural die and total. Against each observer's passive DC `10 + WIS Perception modifier including applicable proficiency`, use the Core four-degree task system. Meet DC succeeds before natural1/20 degree adjustment; Success/Critical Success establishes Hidden for that observer. No unsourced passive ±5 adjustment for advantage/disadvantage. Actual Search rolls use their normal modifiers. Hidden means that observer cannot see you and does not know your location. Clear sight or learning your position ends Hidden for that observer. Moving between hiding places can retain Hidden only if no clear sight/revealing event occurs. A failed Hide cannot simply be rerolled unchanged; a materially new hiding position, concealment, distraction, sight-breaking event or express reroll feature is required. Hidden is not a generic immunity or a free stealth roll for every move. **Search** Action: WIS Perception for sensory detection; INT Investigation for appropriate traces/deduction; Core Arcana/Engineering where relevant. Directly locating a Hidden creature defaults to Perception against its recorded final Stealth total as a static DC, using task degrees/natural shifts. Success reveals location only to the searcher and ends Hidden for that observer, without automatically granting sight. Communicate normally to inform others. Search can be repeated on later turns by paying the Action. Attacks against unseen targets have disadvantage; unseen attackers have advantage, subject to the **Core Invisible exception**: once its position is known, Invisible's own attack modifiers end for that observer and cannot immediately return solely because it remains Invisible. Independent darkness, smoke, blindness or hiding can still supply modifiers. Resolve an attack using pre-attack information; afterward reveal location to observers able to perceive its evidence. If an unseen target's location is unknown, do not directly choose it for a creature-target effect. A weapon/projectile can instead attack a suspected space: wrong space automatically misses; correct space uses unseen rules. A “creature you can see” requirement is never met by position knowledge alone. Area effects can target legal points under their own rules. ## Scope and validation [Reference checks](COMBAT_PLAYTEST_VALIDATION.json) exercise selected budgets, clocks and edge cases. They do not implement map visibility, inventory, feature legality, complete event dispatch, all maneuvers or a live application. No claim of encounter balance or human playtest completion follows from a passing report. `start_life_sequence` receives already-resolved/arbitrated HP events; it does not perform condition-family arbitration, cure processing, trigger generation, duration expiry, target legality, defense calculations or general simultaneous-event dispatch. `hide_results` answers only whether Hidden is established, collapsing the two success degrees and two failure degrees; it does not retain the full task-degree record. The Reaction helper models the ordinary resource, not additional Reaction grants printed on specific cards. Remaining work includes cargo terminology/capacity/durability; detailed movement/environment/chases; area templates and sensory abilities; summons/boss full-turn examples; all-source class/spell semantic interactions and specific resource grants; and the whole-game ledger's other acceptance gates. --- Falling bridge --- avel to a known HQ on the same or another plane. Its positive-standing predicate uses the current canonical reputation record without migrating its scale. Travel may bypass a geographic route; elapsed clocks, cargo custody, contamination, claims, arrival law and settlement remain actual procedures. These cards do not declare automatic Extraction success or invent a blanket prohibition on planar escape. [Review status](CONJURATION_RANK3_REVIEW_STATUS.json) preserves the manuscript/runtime/balance distinction. Noncombat helpers cannot attack or operate combat items. A cantrip that stabilizes restores no HP and removes no Wound. Mending cannot replace finite machine repair or refresh ammunition, charges, duration or class resources. Created tools, servants and water obey the explicit card limits; a spell does not silently pay wages, produce saleable materials or erase abstract expedition Supply. All hard whole-Action/turn denial uses one combined **source-character/target/encounter** record across that character's spells, class features and companions. Once used, later hard-denial riders from that source fail; damage and soft riders resolve as printed. Boss immunities remain effective. Same-stat flat bonuses use the strongest; same-name dice bonuses do not stack. Apply one allowed bonus-die spell per roll in this packet unless a reviewed feature explicitly permits more. Temporary HP never add together. Copies never generate copies, class procs or resource loops. ## Falling bridge — new provisional procedure This is a newly authored table procedure agreed with the GPT notebook, **not recovered V6/Core V2 canon**. Focused searches of the current rulebook extract, Core V2 and mechanics/manual text found spell summaries but no operational falling clock or impact formula. Before promotion to canon, inspect the approved V6 environment/travel/hazard sections and archived environment tables. Preserve any older rule found and compare it explicitly. When a creature loses support with no movement mode or effect keeping it aloft, it enters **Falling** immediately. Falling means unsupported; it grants no hovering, airborne walking or lateral movement. Track total distance descended during this fall and cumulative distance during its current uncontrolled portion, both initially zero. **Timing:** If a fall begins during a regular initiative turn, set a fixed Fall Marker at the **end of that initiative slot in the following round**. Resolve the first Falling Step there, then one at the same marker each round. Reactions and extra turns use the enclosing regular initiative slot; an event between slots uses the most recently completed slot. Extra turns, extra Actions and initiative changes neither move nor multiply the marker. This gives every participant one normal turn opportunity before the first descent; inability to act still matters and no free rescue action is granted. A caster still needs legal targets within range when spending their Action. Outside initiative, when timing matters, establish an order before descent and use a virtual slot immediately before its first regular slot as the initiating slot. The first step occurs when that virtual slot next recurs, after everyone has had one normal turn opportunity. When no rescue or other timing decision is possible, the Director can resolve the same descent and impact calculation directly. This is an explicit timing abstraction, not a model of physical acceleration. If a fall begins after initiative has been established but before any regular initiative slot has yet completed, treat the virtual pre-round slot as the most recently completed slot for Fall Marker purposes. **Ordinary descent:** At each step descend up to **300 feet**, stopping on a supporting surface. Add the distance to both trackers. On landing while uncontrolled, take **min(10, floor(uncontrolled distance / 20))d6 bludgeoning damage**. The uncontrolled distance includes earlier steps; never use only the final step's remainder. **Feather Fall:** The spell retains its **Action** casting time and reduces descent to at most **60 feet per step**. When it takes effect, set uncontrolled distance to zero: the magic dissipates prior uncontrolled momentum in this abstraction. Keep total fall distance. If landing while it remains active, damage is **min(3, floor(total fall distance / 20))d6 bludgeoning**. This explicitly limits impact exposure without guaranteeing a harmless landing. When the spell ends aloft, reset uncontrolled distance to zero, then accumulate ordinary descent from that point at the existing marker; expiry grants no immediate extra step. Its one-minute maximum ends immediately before the caster's regular turn ten rounds after casting. If expiry and a step coincide, resolve expiry first. Earlier landing or cessation of Falling ends the spell on that target as printed. Apply damage defenses only when their actual wording covers that damage. After damage, Falling ends; a creature suffering any positive falling damage lands **Prone** unless an explicit feature prevents it. Less than 20 relevant feet produces zero damage. Forced movement that removes support starts a fall but adds no separate fall damage. Regaining genuine support, secure support from a hold, or flight ends Falling without resolving an uncompleted impact; teleporting safely onto support likewise ends it. A later loss of support begins a new fall with new trackers and marker. Completed impact and damage cannot be undone by later casting. | Scenario | Steps to land | Impact | |---|---:|---:| | Ordinary 10 feet | 1 | 0 | | Ordinary 20 feet | 1 | 1d6 | | Ordinary 300 feet | 1 | 10d6 | | Ordinary 310 feet | 2 | 10d6 | | Ordinary 610 feet | 3 | 10d6 | | Feather Fall active through a 310-foot fall | 6 | 3d6 | | 120 feet slowed, spell ends aloft, then 190 feet uncontrolled | 3 | 9d6 | The 300-foot ordinary step, impact formulas, momentum reset, marker and duration conventions are provisional inventions. The source supplies Feather Fall's Action, five targets and 60-foot slowed descent. Wider hazards, equipment catches, flight loss and environmental damage still need the combat/campaign pass. ## Recovery bridge and unresolved integration The class suite's **Retune** formula is the active RP rule for this combined test: recover max(0, floor(maxRP/4 + 0.5) + actual casting modifier), capped at maximum, and clear Dissonance after a completed one-hour short rest. Do not split one packet to multiply Wound saves or combine separate hits merely to cross a threshold. Feature-specific prevention that acts before damage or before reaching zero resolves at its printed timing first. 1. Resolve attack/save, damage and applicable defenses. The remaining nonnegative amount is **resolved damage**. 2. Record HP, Wounds and zero-HP state before the event. Remove temporary HP first. **Post-temp damage = resolved damage minus temporary HP absorbed.** Keep this amount even when it exceeds current HP; floor the resulting current HP at zero. 3. Check V6 Instant Death. If post-temp damage minus HP before the hit is at least maximum HP, the creature dies. For a creature already at zero, this is post-temp damage at least maximum HP. Stop: there is no Wound save, death-save failure or successful knockout from that event. 4. Otherwise, a living creature makes one Wound save if it dropped from positive HP to zero, if **2 × post-temp damage is at least maximum HP**, or if the effect actually inflicts Maiming. Overlapping triggers still cause only one save. The post-temp threshold and one-save rule are new provisional interpretations. Maiming is an independent trigger even when temporary HP absorbs all ordinary damage; immunity to Maiming itself still applies. 5. Make a Constitution save against **max(10, floor(post-temp damage / 2))**. Use pre-event Wounds for the -1-per-Wound penalty. Maiming with zero post-temp damage uses DC 10. A Wound gained now cannot penalize the roll that created it. 6. A success adds no Wound. A failure that began with zero, one or two Wounds adds exactly one; failure by 5 or more also gives that Wound one Injury. A failure that began with three Wounds kills the creature instead: no fourth Wound and no Injury roll. Stop processing later living-target consequences if dead. 7. If the creature was already at zero before this event and post-temp damage is positive, end Stable. A creature using death saves adds one failure, or two for a critical hit; three failures kill it. An ordinary creature that does not use death saves dies instead, without starting death counters. A hit from positive HP to zero does not also cause a same-packet death failure. 8. If this event newly reduces positive HP to zero, a valid melee knockout makes the surviving creature Stable. Otherwise a character/significant NPC using death saves begins dying; an ordinary monster dies. A previously Stable creature whose temporary HP absorbed all damage remains Stable unless an independent Maiming result killed it. The Director identifies significant NPCs consistently. Core's condition already includes Incapacitated, unawareness, dropping held items and falling Prone; an adjacent hit is critical as specified there. Healing does not silently remove other independent causes of Unconscious or Prone. While dying, make one death save at the **start of each regular initiative turn**. Roll d20 without modifiers unless a feature specifically modifies death saves. Natural 20 restores 1 HP; natural 1 adds two failures. Other results of 10 or more add one success; 9 or less add one failure. Three successes make the creature Stable; three failures make it Dead. Extra turns and Actions add no extra death saves. Stable or Dead creatures do not roll them. Regaining positive HP or becoming Stable resets both counters. Temporary HP does not raise normal HP, end Unconscious or reset counters. Fully absorbed damage does not end Stable or add a death failure in this provisional interpretation. Independent Maiming can still cause its Wound save. **Stabilize:** an Action and DC 10 Wisdom (Medicine) check, or an Action using one healer's-kit use automatically, stabilizes a living dying creature at zero. It remains Unconscious and makes no death saves. Roll 1d4 once when it becomes Stable and record its absolute recovery time that many hours later. If still Stable then, it regains 1 HP. Damage reaching normal HP cancels that deadline and resumes dying with the appropriate failures. Rest and Stable recovery use the same elapsed clock, so four hours of recovery inside an eight-hour Watch do not cost twelve hours. A later rest does not separately replay the Stable healing event. **Dead:** ordinary healing and stabilization do not restore life. Resurrection must satisfy its own willing-soul, body, time, cost and target rules. It does not automatically remove Wounds, Corruption, debts, legal death or claims upon the body. Corpses cannot supply repeated resource triggers. ## Wounds and Critical state"
